## Idea

The {{#concept "cumulative hierarchy" WD="cumulative hierarchy" WDID=Q18349448}}
is a model of [set theory](set-theory.md). Instead of introducing it as a HIT,
as in Section 10.4 of {{#cite UF13}}, we introduce its induction principle
following {{#cite dJKFX23}}.

## Definitions

### Pseudo cumulative hierarchy

A type is a pseudo cumulative hierarchy if it has the structure of a cumulative
hierarchy, but not necessarily its induction principle.

<pre class="Agda"><a id="has-cumulative-hierarchy-structure"></a><a id="2085" href="set-theory.cumulative-hierarchy.html#2085" class="Function">has-cumulative-hierarchy-structure</a> <a id="2120" class="Symbol">:</a>

<a id="2170" href="set-theory.cumulative-hierarchy.html#2085" class="Function">has-cumulative-hierarchy-structure</a> <a id="2205" class="Symbol">{</a><a id="2206" href="set-theory.cumulative-hierarchy.html#2206" class="Bound">l</a><a id="2207" class="Symbol">}</a> <a id="2209" href="set-theory.cumulative-hierarchy.html#2209" class="Bound">V</a> <a id="2211" class="Symbol">=</a>
  <a id="2215" class="Symbol">(</a> <a id="2217" href="foundation-core.sets.html#847" class="Function">is-set</a> <a id="2224" href="set-theory.cumulative-hierarchy.html#2209" class="Bound">V</a><a id="2225" class="Symbol">)</a> <a id="2227" href="foundation-core.cartesian-product-types.html#585" class="Function Operator">×</a>

Another simplification of the recursion principle, when recursive calls are not
needed.
